tom_inf_bat_open_designer_amphibious_mechanized_effect = {
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_battalion_amphibious_mechanized_company_array
		value = temp_v # Avoid any potential name overlap
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_battalion_company_array = temp_v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_battalion_amphibious_mechanized_platoon_array
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_battalion_platoon_array = temp_v }
	}
	
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_base_stats_array
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_popup_base_stats_array = temp_v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_combat_stats_array
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_popup_combat_stats_array = temp_v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_equipment_stats_array_1
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_popup_equipment_stats_array_1 = temp_v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_equipment_stats_array_2_type
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_popup_equipment_stats_array_2_type = temp_v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_equipment_stats_array_2_count
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_popup_equipment_stats_array_2_count = temp_v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_equipment_stats_array_2_count
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_popup_equipment_stats_array_2_needed = temp_v }
	}
	
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_bonus_array
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_popup_terrain_bonus_array = temp_v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_movement_array
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_popup_terrain_movement_array = temp_v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_attack_array
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_popup_terrain_attack_array = temp_v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_defense_array
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_popup_terrain_defense_array = temp_v }
	}
	
	tom_inf_bat_open_support_amphibious_mechanized_effect = yes
	tom_inf_bat_open_subunit_counts_amphibious_mechanized_effect = yes	
}

tom_inf_bat_save_designer_amphibious_mechanized_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				tom_inf_save_button_increase_amphibious_mechanized_trigger = yes
				tom_inf_save_button_decrease_amphibious_mechanized_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		tom_inf_bat_add_effect_amphibious_mechanized = yes
		tom_inf_bat_remove_effect_amphibious_mechanized = yes
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { tom_inf_bat_popup_platoon_count_org > tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_platoon_count_org }
			}
			tom_inf_bat_increase_platoon_org_amphibious_mechanized = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { tom_inf_bat_popup_platoon_count_org < tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_platoon_count_org }
			}
			tom_inf_bat_decrease_platoon_org_amphibious_mechanized = yes
		}
		
		clear_array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_base_stats_array
		clear_array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_combat_stats_array
		clear_array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_equipment_stats_array_1
		clear_array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_equipment_stats_array_2_type
		clear_array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_equipment_stats_array_2_count
		
		clear_array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_bonus_array
		clear_array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_movement_array
		clear_array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_attack_array
		clear_array = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_defense_array
		
		for_each_loop = {
			array = tom_inf_bat_popup_base_stats_array
			value = temp_v
			add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_base_stats_array = temp_v }
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = tom_inf_bat_popup_combat_stats_array
			value = temp_v
			add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_combat_stats_array = temp_v }
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = tom_inf_bat_popup_equipment_stats_array_1
			value = temp_v
			add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_equipment_stats_array_1 = temp_v }
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = tom_inf_bat_popup_equipment_stats_array_2_type
			value = temp_v
			add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_equipment_stats_array_2_type = temp_v }
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = tom_inf_bat_popup_equipment_stats_array_2_count
			value = temp_v
			add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_equipment_stats_array_2_count = temp_v }
		}
		
		for_each_loop = {
			array = tom_inf_bat_popup_terrain_bonus_array
			value = temp_v
			add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_bonus_array = temp_v }
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = tom_inf_bat_popup_terrain_movement_array
			value = temp_v
			add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_movement_array = temp_v }
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = tom_inf_bat_popup_terrain_attack_array
			value = temp_v
			add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_attack_array = temp_v }
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = tom_inf_bat_popup_terrain_defense_array
			value = temp_v
			add_to_array = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_terrain_defense_array = temp_v }
		}
	}
	
	tom_inf_bat_update_support_amphibious_mechanized_effect = yes
	
	clear_array = tom_inf_battalion_amphibious_mechanized_company_array
	clear_array = tom_inf_battalion_amphibious_mechanized_platoon_array
	
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_battalion_company_array
		value = temp_v # Avoid any potential name overlap
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_battalion_amphibious_mechanized_company_array = temp_v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = tom_inf_battalion_platoon_array
		value = temp_v
		add_to_array = { tom_inf_battalion_amphibious_mechanized_platoon_array = temp_v }
	}
	
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_32_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_32_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_11_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_11_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_12_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_12_count }
	
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_3200_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_3200_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1001_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1002_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1003_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1010_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1010_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1020_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1020_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1021_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1021_count }
	
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1100_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1100_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1101_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1101_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1102_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1102_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1110_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1110_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1111_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1111_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1112_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1112_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1113_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1113_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1120_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1120_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1121_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1121_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1122_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1122_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1130_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1130_count }
	
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1210_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1210_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1211_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1211_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1212_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1212_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1220_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1220_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1221_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1221_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1222_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1222_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1223_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1223_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1230_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1230_count }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1240_count = tom_inf_bat_popup_1240_count }
	
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1100_min = tom_inf_bat_popup_1100_min }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1101_min = tom_inf_bat_popup_1101_min }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1102_min = tom_inf_bat_popup_1102_min }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1110_min = tom_inf_bat_popup_1110_min }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1120_min = tom_inf_bat_popup_1120_min }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1122_min = tom_inf_bat_popup_1122_min }
	
	set_variable = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_platoon_count_org = tom_inf_bat_popup_platoon_count_org }
	
	tom_inf_bat_set_doctrine_variables_amphibious_mechanized_effect = yes
}

tom_inf_bat_set_doctrine_variables_amphibious_mechanized_effect = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_all = 0 }
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1001
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1001_count }
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1001_count }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_all = tom_inf_bat_popup_1001_count }
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1002
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1002_count }
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1002_count }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_all = tom_inf_bat_popup_1002_count }
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1003
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1003_count }
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1003_count }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_all = tom_inf_bat_popup_1003_count }
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1010
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1010_count }
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1010_count }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_all = tom_inf_bat_popup_1010_count }
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1020
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1020_count }
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1020_count }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_all = tom_inf_bat_popup_1020_count }
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1021
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1021_count }
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_support = tom_inf_bat_popup_1021_count }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_bonus_all = tom_inf_bat_popup_1021_count }
	}
	
	set_variable = { tom_inf_doctrine_mastery_support_amphibious_mechanized = doctrine_bonus_support }
	set_variable = { tom_inf_doctrine_mastery_all_amphibious_mechanized = doctrine_bonus_all }
}

tom_inf_bat_open_support_amphibious_mechanized_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1001
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1001
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1002
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1002
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1003
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1003
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1010
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1010
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1020
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1020
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1021
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1021
	}
}

tom_inf_bat_update_support_amphibious_mechanized_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1001
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1001
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1002
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1002
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1003
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1003
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1010
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1010
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1020
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1020
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1021
		}
		set_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_amphibious_mechanized_1021
	}
}

tom_inf_bat_add_effect_amphibious_mechanized = {
	# Adds necessary techs after saving a larger Battalion. Performance intensive due to fuckload of triggers, so use only when needed
	# !!Only enter valid Techs!! Will crash the game otherwise
	
	### COMPANIES
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { tom_inf_bat_popup_32_count > 3 }
		}
		# Enlarge Support Column for the 4th Rifle Company
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1001
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1001
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech2_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech2_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1001
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech2_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech3_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech3_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1001
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech3_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech4_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech4_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1001
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech4_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech5_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech5_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1001
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech5_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech6_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1001_tech6_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1002
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1002
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech2_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech2_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1002
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech2_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech3_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech3_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1002
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech3_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech4_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech4_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1002
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech4_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech5_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech5_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1002
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech5_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech6_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1002_tech6_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1003
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1003
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech2_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech2_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1003
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech2_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech3_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech3_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1003
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech3_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech4_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech4_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = tom_inf_bat_support_1003
				has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech4_reset_4
				NOT = { has_tech = tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech5_4 }
			}
			set_technology = { tom_inf_bat_amphibious_mechanized_1003_tech5_4 = 1 popup = no }
		}
		else_if = {
